Let x_2 = 1 + p.

  Have: (2 <= p) /\ (p <= x).
  (* Invariant 'i_12' *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. let x_3 = is_p_0[i_3] in ((i_3 <= n) ->
      ((2 <= i_3) -> ((x_3 = 0) \/ (x_3 = 1)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_13' *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. ((i_3 <= n) -> ((2 <= i_3) -> (P_is_prime(i_3) ->
      (is_p_0[i_3] = 1)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_14' *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. ((is_p_0[i_3] = 1) -> ((i_3 <= n) -> ((i_3 < x_1) ->
      ((2 <= i_3) -> P_is_prime(i_3))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_15' *)
  Have: forall i_4,i_3 : Z. ((i_3 < p) -> ((i_4 <= n) ->
      (((i_3 * i_3) <= i_4) -> ((2 <= i_3) -> (P_divides(i_3, i_4) ->
      (is_p_0[i_4] = 0)))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_24' *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. ((i_3 <= n) -> ((i_3 < (x_2 * x_2)) -> ((2 <= i_3) ->
      ((!P_is_prime(i_3)) ->
      (exists i_4 : Z. (i_4 <= p) /\ ((i_4 * i_4) <= i_3) /\ (2 <= i_4) /\
       P_divides(i_4, i_3)))))).
  (* Else *)
  Have: n < x_1.
  (* Assertion *)
  Have: is_p_0[2] = 1.
  (* Assertion *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. ((is_p_0[i_3] = 1) -> ((i_3 <= n) -> ((2 <= i_3) ->
      P_is_prime(i_3)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_2' *)
  Have: (2 <= i_1) /\ (i_1 <= x).
  (* Invariant 'i_3' *)
  Have: (0 <= cnt_0) /\ ((2 + cnt_0) <= i_1).
  (* Invariant 'i_4' *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. let a_4 = a_1[shift_sint32(primes_0, i_3)] in
      ((0 <= i_3) -> ((i_3 < cnt_0) -> ((a_4 < i_1) /\ (2 <= a_4)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_5' *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. ((0 <= i_3) -> ((i_3 < cnt_0) ->
      (a_1[shift_sint32(primes_0, i_3)] <= n))).
  (* Invariant 'i_6' *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. ((0 <= i_3) -> ((i_3 < cnt_0) ->
      (is_p_0[a_1[shift_sint32(primes_0, i_3)]] = 1))).
  (* Invariant 'i_7' *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. ((0 <= i_3) -> ((i_3 < cnt_0) ->
      P_is_prime(a_1[shift_sint32(primes_0, i_3)]))).
  (* Invariant 'i_8' *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. ((is_p_0[i_3] = 1) -> ((i_3 < i_1) -> ((2 <= i_3) ->
      (exists i_4 : Z. (a_1[shift_sint32(primes_0, i_4)] = i_3) /\
       (0 <= i_4) /\ (i_4 < cnt_0))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_9' *)
  Have: forall i_4,i_3 : Z. ((0 <= i_4) -> ((i_4 < i_3) -> ((i_3 < cnt_0) ->
      (a_1[shift_sint32(primes_0, i_4)] < a_1[shift_sint32(primes_0, i_3)])))).
  (* Invariant 'i_10' *)
  Have: ((3 <= i_1) -> ((a_2 = 2) /\ (0 < cnt_0))).
  (* Else *)
  Have: n < i_1.
}
Prove: (a_3 <= n) /\ (2 <= a_3).

  Have: forall i_3 : Z. ((is_p_3[i_3] = 1) -> ((i_3 <= n) -> ((i_3 < x) ->
      ((2 <= i_3) -> P_is_prime(i_3))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_15' *)
  Have: forall i_4,i_3 : Z. ((i_3 < p) -> ((i_4 <= n) ->
      (((i_3 * i_3) <= i_4) -> ((2 <= i_3) -> (P_divides(i_3, i_4) ->
      (is_p_3[i_4] = 0)))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_24' *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. ((i_3 <= n) -> ((i_3 < (x_2 * x_2)) -> ((2 <= i_3) ->
      ((!P_is_prime(i_3)) ->
      (exists i_4 : Z. (i_4 <= p) /\ ((i_4 * i_4) <= i_3) /\ (2 <= i_4) /\
       P_divides(i_4, i_3)))))).
  (* Then *)
  Have: x <= n.
  (* Invariant 'i_16' *)
  Have: p <= n.
  (* Invariant 'i_17' *)
  Have: x <= x_1.
  (* Invariant 'i_18' *)
  Have: P_divides(p, x).
  (* Loop assigns ... *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. ((0 <= i_3) -> ((i_3 <= 100) ->
      (((n < i_3) \/ (i_3 < x)) -> (is_p_3[i_3] = is_p_0[i_3])))).
  (* Invariant 'i_17' *)
  Have: (x <= i_1) /\ (i_1 <= x_1).
  (* Invariant 'i_18' *)
  Have: P_divides(p, i_1).
  (* Invariant 'i_19' *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. let x_5 = is_p_0[i_3] in ((i_3 <= n) ->
      ((2 <= i_3) -> ((x_5 = 0) \/ (x_5 = 1)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_20' *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. ((i_3 <= n) -> ((2 <= i_3) -> (P_is_prime(i_3) ->
      (is_p_0[i_3] = 1)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_21' *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. ((is_p_0[i_3] = 1) -> ((i_3 <= n) -> ((i_3 < x) ->
      ((2 <= i_3) -> P_is_prime(i_3))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_22' *)
  Have: forall i_4,i_3 : Z. ((i_3 < p) -> ((i_4 <= n) ->
      (((i_3 * i_3) <= i_4) -> ((2 <= i_3) -> (P_divides(i_3, i_4) ->
      (is_p_0[i_4] = 0)))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_23' *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. ((i_3 < i_1) -> ((x <= i_3) -> (P_divides(p, i_3) ->
      (is_p_0[i_3] = 0)))).
  (* Else *)
  Have: n < i_1.
  (* Invariant 'i_11' *)
  Have: 0 < p.
}
Prove: (x_4 = 0) \/ (x_4 = 1).

      (((n < i_4) \/ (i_4 <= 1)) -> (is_p_2[i_4] = is_p_3[i_4])))).
  (* Invariant 'i_11' *)
  Have: (2 <= p) /\ (p <= x_4).
  (* Invariant 'i_12' *)
  Have: forall i_4 : Z. let x_5 = is_p_3[i_4] in ((i_4 <= n) ->
      ((2 <= i_4) -> ((x_5 = 0) \/ (x_5 = 1)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_13' *)
  Have: forall i_4 : Z. ((i_4 <= n) -> ((2 <= i_4) -> (P_is_prime(i_4) ->
      (is_p_3[i_4] = 1)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_14' *)
  Have: forall i_4 : Z. ((is_p_3[i_4] = 1) -> ((i_4 <= n) -> ((i_4 < x_2) ->
      ((2 <= i_4) -> P_is_prime(i_4))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_15' *)
  Have: forall i_5,i_4 : Z. ((i_4 < p) -> ((i_5 <= n) ->
      (((i_4 * i_4) <= i_5) -> ((2 <= i_4) -> (P_divides(i_4, i_5) ->
      (is_p_3[i_5] = 0)))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_24' *)
  Have: forall i_4 : Z. ((i_4 <= n) -> ((i_4 < x_1) -> ((2 <= i_4) ->
      ((!P_is_prime(i_4)) ->
      (exists i_5 : Z. (i_5 <= p) /\ ((i_5 * i_5) <= i_4) /\ (2 <= i_5) /\
       P_divides(i_5, i_4)))))).
  (* Then *)
  Have: x_2 <= n.
  (* Invariant 'i_16' *)
  Have: p <= n.
  (* Invariant 'i_17' *)
  Have: x_2 <= x_3.
  (* Invariant 'i_18' *)
  Have: P_divides(p, x_2).
  (* Loop assigns ... *)
  Have: forall i_4 : Z. ((0 <= i_4) -> ((i_4 <= 100) ->
      (((n < i_4) \/ (i_4 < x_2)) -> (is_p_3[i_4] = is_p_0[i_4])))).
  (* Invariant 'i_17' *)
  Have: (x_2 <= i_1) /\ (i_1 <= x_3).
  (* Invariant 'i_18' *)
  Have: P_divides(p, i_1).
  (* Invariant 'i_19' *)
  Have: forall i_4 : Z. let x_5 = is_p_0[i_4] in ((i_4 <= n) ->
      ((2 <= i_4) -> ((x_5 = 0) \/ (x_5 = 1)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_20' *)
  Have: forall i_4 : Z. ((i_4 <= n) -> ((2 <= i_4) -> (P_is_prime(i_4) ->
      (is_p_0[i_4] = 1)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_21' *)
  Have: forall i_4 : Z. ((is_p_0[i_4] = 1) -> ((i_4 <= n) -> ((i_4 < x_2) ->
      ((2 <= i_4) -> P_is_prime(i_4))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_22' *)
  Have: forall i_5,i_4 : Z. ((i_4 < p) -> ((i_5 <= n) ->
      (((i_4 * i_4) <= i_5) -> ((2 <= i_4) -> (P_divides(i_4, i_5) ->
      (is_p_0[i_5] = 0)))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_23' *)
  Have: forall i_4 : Z. ((i_4 < i_1) -> ((x_2 <= i_4) ->
      (P_divides(p, i_4) -> (is_p_0[i_4] = 0)))).
  (* Else *)
  Have: n < i_1.
  (* Invariant 'i_11' *)
  Have: 0 < p.
  (* Invariant 'i_14' *)

      ((2 <= i_4) -> P_is_prime(i_4))))).
}

Goal Establishment of Invariant 'i_17' (file /results/test-03-01/deepseek-flash/test-03-01-45.cbs/.col2inv-trustc-wsfaad3q.c, line 80):
Let x = p * p.
Let x_1 = 1 + p.
Let x_2 = 1 + n.
Assume {
  Type: is_sint32(i) /\ is_sint32(n) /\ is_sint32(p) /\ is_sint32(x).
  (* Heap *)
  Type: (region(primes_0.base) <= 0) /\ linked(Malloc_0).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: (2 <= n) /\ (n <= 100).
  (* Pre-condition *)
  Have: valid_rw(Malloc_0, shift_sint32(primes_0, 0), n).
  (* Invariant 'i_0' *)
  Have: 0 < n.
  (* Loop assigns ... *)
  Have: forall i_1 : Z. ((0 <= i_1) -> ((i_1 <= 100) ->
      (((n < i_1) \/ (i_1 <= 1)) -> (is_p_0[i_1] = is_p_1[i_1])))).
  (* Invariant 'i_0' *)
  Have: (2 <= i) /\ (i <= x_2).
  (* Invariant 'i_1' *)
  Have: forall i_1 : Z. ((i_1 < i) -> ((2 <= i_1) -> (is_p_1[i_1] = 1))).
  (* Else *)
  Have: n < i.
  (* Invariant 'i_12' *)
  Have: forall i_1 : Z. let x_3 = is_p_1[i_1] in ((i_1 <= n) ->
      ((2 <= i_1) -> ((x_3 = 0) \/ (x_3 = 1)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_13' *)
  Have: forall i_1 : Z. ((i_1 <= n) -> ((2 <= i_1) -> (P_is_prime(i_1) ->
      (is_p_1[i_1] = 1)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_14' *)
  Have: forall i_1 : Z. ((is_p_1[i_1] = 1) -> ((i_1 <= n) -> ((2 <= i_1) ->
      ((i_1 <= 3) -> P_is_prime(i_1))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_24' *)
  Have: forall i_1 : Z. ((i_1 <= n) -> ((2 <= i_1) -> ((i_1 <= 8) ->
      ((!P_is_prime(i_1)) -> ((4 <= i_1) /\ P_divides(2, i_1)))))).
  (* Loop assigns ... *)
  Have: forall i_1 : Z. ((0 <= i_1) -> ((i_1 <= 100) ->
      (((n < i_1) \/ (i_1 <= 1)) -> (is_p_1[i_1] = is_p_2[i_1])))).
  (* Invariant 'i_11' *)
  Have: (2 <= p) /\ (p <= x_2).
  (* Invariant 'i_12' *)
  Have: forall i_1 : Z. let x_3 = is_p_2[i_1] in ((i_1 <= n) ->
      ((2 <= i_1) -> ((x_3 = 0) \/ (x_3 = 1)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_13' *)
  Have: forall i_1 : Z. ((i_1 <= n) -> ((2 <= i_1) -> (P_is_prime(i_1) ->
      (is_p_2[i_1] = 1)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_14' *)
  Have: forall i_1 : Z. ((is_p_2[i_1] = 1) -> ((i_1 <= n) -> ((i_1 < x) ->
      ((2 <= i_1) -> P_is_prime(i_1))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_15' *)
  Have: forall i_2,i_1 : Z. ((i_1 < p) -> ((i_2 <= n) ->
      (((i_1 * i_1) <= i_2) -> ((2 <= i_1) -> (P_divides(i_1, i_2) ->
      (is_p_2[i_2] = 0)))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_24' *)
  Have: forall i_1 : Z. ((i_1 <= n) -> ((i_1 < (x_1 * x_1)) -> ((2 <= i_1) ->
      ((!P_is_prime(i_1)) ->
      (exists i_2 : Z. (i_2 <= p) /\ ((i_2 * i_2) <= i_1) /\ (2 <= i_2) /\
       P_divides(i_2, i_1)))))).
  (* Then *)
  Have: x <= n.
  (* Invariant 'i_16' *)
  Have: p <= n.
}
Prove: x <= (n + p).

  Have: forall i_2 : Z. ((0 <= i_2) -> ((i_2 <= 100) ->
      (((n < i_2) \/ (i_2 <= 1)) -> (is_p_2[i_2] = is_p_0[i_2])))).
  (* Invariant 'i_11' *)
  Have: (2 <= p) /\ (p <= x_2).
  (* Invariant 'i_12' *)
  Have: forall i_2 : Z. let x_4 = is_p_0[i_2] in ((i_2 <= n) ->
      ((2 <= i_2) -> ((x_4 = 0) \/ (x_4 = 1)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_13' *)
  Have: forall i_2 : Z. ((i_2 <= n) -> ((2 <= i_2) -> (P_is_prime(i_2) ->
      (is_p_0[i_2] = 1)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_14' *)
  Have: forall i_2 : Z. ((is_p_0[i_2] = 1) -> ((i_2 <= n) -> ((i_2 < x) ->
      ((2 <= i_2) -> P_is_prime(i_2))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_15' *)
  Have: forall i_3,i_2 : Z. ((i_2 < p) -> ((i_3 <= n) ->
      (((i_2 * i_2) <= i_3) -> ((2 <= i_2) -> (P_divides(i_2, i_3) ->
      (is_p_0[i_3] = 0)))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_24' *)
  Have: forall i_2 : Z. ((i_2 <= n) -> ((i_2 < (x_1 * x_1)) -> ((2 <= i_2) ->
      ((!P_is_prime(i_2)) ->
      (exists i_3 : Z. (i_3 <= p) /\ ((i_3 * i_3) <= i_2) /\ (2 <= i_3) /\
       P_divides(i_3, i_2)))))).
  (* Then *)
  Have: x <= n.
  (* Invariant 'i_16' *)
  Have: p <= n.
  (* Invariant 'i_17' *)
  Have: x <= (n + p).
  (* Invariant 'i_18' *)
  Have: P_divides(p, x).
}
Prove: (x_3 = 0) \/ (x_3 = 1).

  Have: forall i_3,i_2 : Z. ((i_2 < p) -> ((i_3 <= n) ->
      (((i_2 * i_2) <= i_3) -> ((2 <= i_2) -> (P_divides(i_2, i_3) ->
      (is_p_0[i_3] = 0)))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_24' *)
  Have: forall i_2 : Z. ((i_2 <= n) -> ((i_2 < (x_1 * x_1)) -> ((2 <= i_2) ->
      ((!P_is_prime(i_2)) ->
      (exists i_3 : Z. (i_3 <= p) /\ ((i_3 * i_3) <= i_2) /\ (2 <= i_3) /\
       P_divides(i_3, i_2)))))).
  (* Then *)
  Have: x <= n.
  (* Invariant 'i_16' *)
  Have: p <= n.
  (* Invariant 'i_17' *)
  Have: x <= (n + p).
  (* Invariant 'i_18' *)
  Have: P_divides(p, x).
}

      ((2 <= i_4) -> ((x_6 = 0) \/ (x_6 = 1)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_13' *)
  Have: forall i_4 : Z. ((i_4 <= n) -> ((2 <= i_4) -> (P_is_prime(i_4) ->
      (is_p_2[i_4] = 1)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_14' *)
  Have: forall i_4 : Z. ((is_p_2[i_4] = 1) -> ((i_4 <= n) -> ((i_4 < x_2) ->
      ((2 <= i_4) -> P_is_prime(i_4))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_15' *)

      (((i + i_4) < i_1) -> ((2 <= i_4) -> (P_divides(i_4, i_5) ->
      (is_p_2[i_5] = 0)))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_24' *)

      (is_p_3[i_4] = 1)))).

      ((2 <= i_4) -> P_is_prime(i_4))))).

      P_is_prime(a_1[shift_sint32(primes_0, i_2)]))).

  Have: forall i_2 : Z. ((is_p_0[i_2] = 1) -> ((i_2 < i) -> ((2 <= i_2) ->

      ((2 <= i_3) -> ((x_5 = 0) \/ (x_5 = 1)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_13' *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. ((i_3 <= n) -> ((2 <= i_3) -> (P_is_prime(i_3) ->
      (is_p_0[i_3] = 1)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_14' *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. ((is_p_0[i_3] = 1) -> ((i_3 <= n) -> ((i_3 < x_3) ->
      ((2 <= i_3) -> P_is_prime(i_3))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_15' *)
  Have: forall i_4,i_3 : Z. ((i_3 < p) -> ((i_4 <= n) ->
      (((i_3 * i_3) <= i_4) -> ((2 <= i_3) -> (P_divides(i_3, i_4) ->
      (is_p_0[i_4] = 0)))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_24' *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. ((i_3 <= n) -> ((i_3 < (x_4 * x_4)) -> ((2 <= i_3) ->
      ((!P_is_prime(i_3)) ->
      (exists i_4 : Z. (i_4 <= p) /\ ((i_4 * i_4) <= i_3) /\ (2 <= i_4) /\
       P_divides(i_4, i_3)))))).
  (* Else *)
  Have: n < x_3.
  (* Assertion *)
  Have: is_p_0[2] = 1.
  (* Assertion *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. ((is_p_0[i_3] = 1) -> ((i_3 <= n) -> ((2 <= i_3) ->
      P_is_prime(i_3)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_2' *)
  Have: (2 <= i_1) /\ (i_1 <= x_2).
  (* Invariant 'i_3' *)
  Have: (0 <= cnt_2) /\ ((2 + cnt_2) <= i_1).
  (* Invariant 'i_4' *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. let a_3 = a_1[shift_sint32(primes_0, i_3)] in
      ((0 <= i_3) -> ((i_3 < cnt_2) -> ((a_3 < i_1) /\ (2 <= a_3)))).
  (* Invariant 'i_5' *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. ((0 <= i_3) -> ((i_3 < cnt_2) ->
      (a_1[shift_sint32(primes_0, i_3)] <= n))).
  (* Invariant 'i_6' *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. ((0 <= i_3) -> ((i_3 < cnt_2) ->
      (is_p_0[a_1[shift_sint32(primes_0, i_3)]] = 1))).
  (* Invariant 'i_7' *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. ((0 <= i_3) -> ((i_3 < cnt_2) ->
      P_is_prime(a_1[shift_sint32(primes_0, i_3)]))).
  (* Invariant 'i_8' *)
  Have: forall i_3 : Z. ((is_p_0[i_3] = 1) -> ((i_3 < i_1) -> ((2 <= i_3) ->
      (exists i_4 : Z. (a_1[shift_sint32(primes_0, i_4)] = i_3) /\
       (0 <= i_4) /\ (i_4 < cnt_2))))).
  (* Invariant 'i_9' *)
  Have: forall i_4,i_3 : Z. ((0 <= i_4) -> ((i_4 < i_3) -> ((i_3 < cnt_2) ->
      (a_1[shift_sint32(primes_0, i_4)] < a_1[shift_sint32(primes_0, i_3)])))).

    (* Assertion *)
    Have: P_is_prime(i_1).
    (* Assertion *)

        (a_1[shift_sint32(primes_0, i_3)] < i_1))).
    Have: a_1[shift_sint32(primes_0, cnt_1) <- i_1] = Mint_0.
    Have: (1 + cnt_1) = cnt_0.

  Else { Have: (cnt_2 = cnt_0) /\ (a_1 = Mint_0). }

  (* Then *)

  (* Then *)
  Have: x_1 != 0.
  (* Assertion *)
  Have: P_is_prime(i).
  (* Assertion *)
  Have: forall i_2 : Z. ((0 <= i_2) -> (((2 + i_2) <= cnt_0) ->
